*Amit Sarkar Suman Sau,Swagata Mandal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自编码器(AE)的机器学习框架，用于FinFET的高效建模。通过BSIM-CMG校准生成ID-VG数据集，训练自编码器将完整I-V曲线压缩为低维潜空间，并显式引入VDS作为输入特征以捕捉偏置依赖变化。该模型能高精度重构I-V曲线并直接提取VTH、SS、gm等关键器件指标，证明数据驱动紧凑模型可凭借少量训练数据实现高精度，为快速器件表征、建模及电路级仿真提供有力工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的FinFET紧凑建模方法（如基于物理方程的BSIM-CMG模型）在参数提取和校准上耗时耗力，亟需一种数据驱动的高效建模方案，能够从实际表征数据中快速、准确地构建紧凑模型，并自动提取关键器件参数。

Method: (1) 使用BSIM-CMG模型校准生成ID-VG特性数据集；(2) 训练自编码器(AE)将完整I-V曲线压缩到低维潜空间，潜空间隐式编码器件物理特性；(3) 创新性地将VDS作为显式输入特征纳入模型，以捕捉偏置依赖变化；(4) 利用训练好的模型重构完整I-V曲线并直接提取VTH、SS、gm等关键指标。

Result: 模型成功实现了对完整I-V曲线的高精度重构，并能够直接提取阈值电压(VTH)、亚阈值摆幅(SS)和峰值跨导(gm)等关键器件参数，且仅需少量训练数据即可达到高精度。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的紧凑模型可以基于实际表征数据实现高精度建模，且训练数据需求小，为快速器件表征、建模和电路级仿真提供了强有力的工具。

Abstract: This work presents a machine learning framework that leverages an autoencoder (AE) for the efficient modeling of FinFET. We first calibrated a BSIM-CMG model to generate a dataset of current-voltage (ID-VG) characteristics. This data was used to train an autoencoder that compresses full I-V curves into a low-dimensional latent space, which intrinsically encodes key device physics. A key innovation is the explicit incorporation of parameter such as drain to source voltage (VDS) as an input feature, enhancing the model ability to capture bias dependent variation. The trained model successfully reconstructs full I-V curves and directly extracts critical device metrics including threshold voltage (VTH), subthreshold slope (SS), and peak transconductance (gm). This approach demonstrates that data driven compact models, built from actual characterization data, can achieve high accuracy with minimal training data, providing a powerful tool for rapid device characterization, modelling and circuit level simulation.

</details>


### [36] [RAVEN: A Regime-Aware Variable-context Expert Network for Financial Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24062)
*Cheng He,Zhenyu Guan,Xijie Liang,Defu Lian,Jiajia Li,Enhong Chen,Patrick P. C. Lee,Geng Hu,Zehao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RAVEN模型，通过分层次嵌套窗口和累积重要性阈值机制自适应确定每个样本的时间上下文，解决了固定上下文窗口与金融时间序列非平稳、低信噪比、状态依赖等特性不匹配的问题，在股票回报预测和基金销售预测上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列存在非平稳性、极低信噪比和状态依赖的时间依赖关系，而现有SOTA时序模型使用固定上下文窗口，无法匹配时变的最优回溯长度，导致预测性能受限。

Method: RAVEN是一个混合专家框架：1) 构建倒序重要性评分的补丁层级，通过累积重要性阈值机制推导嵌套前缀窗口；2) 每个窗口路由到尺度特异专家；3) 全局压缩表示分支并行处理完整上下文以保持全局时间连贯性；4) 相关性感知加权模块对齐变长专家输出并惩罚余弦相似度。

Result: 在沪深300和标普500的累积对数收益预测任务上，Pearson相关系数分别提升9.2%和20.2%；基金销售预测任务上MSE降低18.2%；在四个PEMS交通基准上16个指标中取得14个最佳结果。

Conclusion: RAVEN通过数据驱动的自适应上下文选择机制有效解决了金融时间序列的固定窗口问题，在金融预测和交通预测等多个领域均表现出显著优越性。

Abstract: Financial time series forecasting presents structural challenges absent from standard benchmarks. Log-returns are non-stationary, exhibit exceptionally low signal-to-noise (SNR) ratios, and are governed by regime-dependent temporal dependencies. We identify a key limitation of state-of-the-art (SOTA) time series models in financial settings. A fixed context window is mismatched to the time-varying optimal look-back of non-stationary price processes. We propose the Regime-Aware Variable-context Expert Network (RAVEN), a Mixture-of-Experts framework designed to adaptively determine the temporal context for each input sample. Instead of relying on a fixed look-back horizon, RAVEN constructs a hierarchy of nested contiguous windows whose lengths are determined by the data itself. Specifically, RAVEN scores patches by learned importance in reverse chronological order and applies the Cumulative Importance Thresholding (CIT) mechanism to derive nested prefix windows, each routed to a scale-specialized expert. A Global Compressed Representation (GCR) branch runs in parallel over the full context, preserving global temporal coherence that local experts cannot guarantee. Because the nested routing induces structured overlap among expert inputs, we introduce a Correlation-Aware Weighting (CAW) to align variable-length expert outputs and penalize pairwise cosine similarity prior to aggregation. Experiments on cumulative log-return prediction (HS300, S&P500) and fund sales forecasting demonstrate that RAVEN achieves SOTA performances, improves Pearson correlation by 9.2% on HS300 and 20.2% on S&P500, and reduces MSE by 18.2% on fund sales forecasting, while achieving the best results in 14 of 16 metrics on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>


### [37] [Blockwise Policy-Drift Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24084)
*Liwen Zheng,Haiyun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的块级策略漂移门控方法（blockwise policy-drift gating），通过计算行为学生与当前学生在采样token路径上的对数概率偏移，并将其聚合为固定块（如64 token）的归一化门控值来重新加权OPD位置损失。该方法在不改变教师目标、教师top-K支持或rollout策略的情况下，将采样token OPD在四个数学推理基准上的平均pass@8从0.4978提升至0.5160。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明，在长序列推理任务中，采样token级别的同策略蒸馏（OPD）存在脆弱性问题，且局部教师支持匹配虽然是一种有效的修复手段，但仍需探索更轻量、更通用的控制信号来提升同策略蒸馏的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出blockwise policy-drift gating方法：在rollout复用场景下，计算行为学生（behavior student）与当前学生（current student）在采样token路径上的log-probability偏移；将这些偏移按固定块（如64 token）或跨度进行聚合；使用去关联化、均值归一化的门控值来重新加权OPD位置损失。该方法不改变教师目标、教师top-K支持或rollout策略。

Result: 在Qwen3数学推理基准上的六种变体实验（统一200步训练预算）中，固定64 token块门控将采样token OPD的平均pass@8从0.4978提升至0.5160（覆盖AIME24、AIME25、MATH500和AMC23四个基准）。在Teacher-TopK/LSM设置下，Block64在四个基准上的平均pass@8为所有训练变体中最优。

Conclusion: 局部旧-当前策略漂移（local old-current policy drift）是一种实用的控制信号，可用于复用的OPD rollouts；块级门控（block-level gating）作为一种简单有效的默认策略，能够提升求解率的鲁棒性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student policy using teacher signals computed on trajectories sampled by the student itself. Recent work shows that sampled-token OPD can be fragile on long-horizon reasoning tasks and that local teacher-support matching is a simple and effective repair. This paper introduces blockwise policy-drift gating, a lightweight student-only old-current drift controller for OPD under rollout reuse. The method computes log-probability shifts between the behavior student and the current student on the sampled token path, aggregates these shifts over fixed blocks or spans, and uses the resulting detached, mean-normalized gates to reweight OPD position losses. It does not change teacher targets, teacher top-K supports, or the rollout policy. In a six-variant Qwen3 math reasoning benchmark with a uniform 200-step training budget for all trained variants, we use pass@8 as the primary problem-level solve-rate metric. Fixed 64-token block gating improves sampled-token OPD mean pass@8 from 0.4978 to 0.5160 across AIME24, AIME25, MATH500, and AMC23. On Teacher-TopK/LSM, Block64 gives the best four-benchmark mean pass@8 among trained students. The results identify local old-current policy drift as a practical control signal for reused OPD rollouts and motivate block-level gating as a simple default for improving solve-rate robustness.

</details>


### [38] [NeuroSonic: Conditional Flow Matching for EEG-to-Speech Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24087)
*Wenhao Gao,Yifan Wang,Yijia Ma,Carl Yang,Wen Li,Chenyu You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NeuroSonic，一种基于条件流匹配的框架用于从头皮脑电图（EEG）重建连续语音。该框架通过学习一个确定性的概率流速度场，在EEG条件下将噪声污染的声学状态转化为干净语音，避免了传统方法中的波形回归不稳定和随机多步生成对伪影的敏感性。在CineBrain和EAV基准上，NeuroSonic在分布真实性、频谱保真度和感知质量上显著优于GAN、扩散和均值流基线，感知质量提升高达26.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG信号弱、空间弥散且高度可变，而语音具有强谐波和时间结构，两者之间的失配导致直接波形回归不稳定，多步随机生成对伪影依赖和受试者变异性敏感。现有GAN、扩散和均值流方法在EEG到语音重建任务中表现不佳，亟需一种更稳定有效的重建方法。

Method: NeuroSonic采用条件流匹配框架，学习一个确定性的概率流速度场，将噪声扰乱的声学状态向干净语音运输。EEG和音频被嵌入到共享的token空间，由时间条件门控Transformer参数化运输常微分方程，显式建模轨迹演化，避免迭代随机采样。

Result: 在CineBrain和EAV数据集上的跨被试评估中，NeuroSonic在分布真实性、频谱保真度和感知质量上均超越GAN、扩散和均值流基线，感知质量提升达26.3%。在伪影严重、条件变异性最大的片段上性能优势最为明显。

Conclusion: 确定性条件运输为EEG驱动的语音重建提供了一种稳定且有效的方案，相比随机生成范式更具鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous speech from scalp electroencephalography (EEG) remains fundamentally challenging. EEG provides a weak, spatially diffuse, and highly variable measurement of distributed cortical activity, whereas speech is organized as a coherent acoustic trajectory with strong harmonic and temporal structure. The resulting mismatch makes waveform regression unstable and causes stochastic multi-step generation to be sensitive to artifact-dependent conditioning and subject variability. We introduce NeuroSonic, a conditional flow-matching framework for EEG-to-speech reconstruction. Instead of predicting waveforms directly or refining them through stochastic denoising, NeuroSonic learns a deterministic probability-flow velocity field that transports a noise-corrupted acoustic state toward clean speech under EEG conditioning. EEG and audio are embedded into a shared token space and processed by a time-conditioned gated Transformer that parameterizes the transport ordinary differential equation. This formulation models trajectory evolution explicitly while avoiding iterative stochastic sampling. We evaluate NeuroSonic on the CineBrain and EAV benchmarks under cross-subject evaluation. Across both datasets, the proposed method improves distributional realism, spectral fidelity, and perceptual quality over representative GAN-, diffusion-, and mean-flow baselines, with up to a 26.3\% gain in overall perceptual quality. The performance gap is most evident in artifact-heavy segments, where conditioning variability is strongest. These findings indicate that deterministic conditional transport provides a stable and effective formulation for EEG-driven speech reconstruction. Code is available at https://github.com/Y-Research-SBU/NeuroSonic/ .

</details>


### [39] [FedUP: One-Shot Federated Unlearning via Centroid-Guided Plug-in Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24113)
*Feihong Nan,Zhengyi Zhong,Pan Wang,Weidong Bao,Xiongtao Zhang,Quan Wen,Ji Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedUP提出了一种基于可插拔过滤器的单次联邦遗忘框架，通过服务端训练的差分隐私保护类中心样本过滤器，在冻结原始模型参数的前提下实现高效遗忘，将延迟从分钟级降至秒级，并支持知识恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦遗忘方法面临非目标知识丢失与高请求延迟之间的两难困境，亟需一种既能精准遗忘目标数据又能保持模型性能的高效方案。

Method: 提出FedUP框架，利用轻量级可插拔过滤器作为"知识漏斗"筛选目标数据；冻结原始模型参数，在服务端使用差分隐私保护的类中心样本训练过滤器；实现单次通信即可完成遗忘操作。

Result: 在多种图像和文本任务上，FedUP有效减少了非目标知识损失，实现了优越的遗忘精度和效率，遗忘延迟从分钟级降至秒级。

Conclusion: FedUP通过可插拔过滤器设计解决了联邦遗忘中的核心困境，兼顾了低知识损失、高效率、可逆性等需求，为联邦遗忘提供了实用且合规的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated unlearning (FU) is critical for complying with legal mandates like the right to be forgotten in decentralized systems, yet current methods face a persistent dilemma between non-target knowledge loss and high request latency. To resolve these issues, we propose FedUP, a one-shot federated unlearning framework utilizing lightweight pluggable filters that act as a "knowledge funnel" to screen out target data while preserving original model performance. By freezing original model parameters and training filters at the server side using differentially private (DP)-protected class centroid samples, FedUP bypasses the need for multi-round client-server communication and complex retraining, reducing unlearning latency from minutes to mere seconds. Additionally, the framework's pluggable architecture ensures inherent reversibility, enabling the seamless restoration of forgotten knowledge by simply removing the filters. Extensive experiments on diverse image and text tasks demonstrate that FedUP effectively reduces non-target knowledge loss and achieves superior unlearning precision and efficiency across various scenarios. Code is available at: https://github.com/suows/FedUP-code.

</details>


### [40] [When Top-1 Fails: Calibrating LoRA Monitors for Masked Diffusion LMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24119)
*Lucky Verma,Pratik Yadav*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 离散扩散语言模型(DLM)的PEFT微调中，基于top-1 argmax浓度的崩溃监测器精度为零（0/816），原因是预平衡饱和；而使用最大LoRA梯度范数作为参数侧信号，在LLaDA系列上对top-decile高损失配置达到0.68精度和0.79 F1。建议放弃top-1指标，改用最大梯度早期监测并按模型系列校准阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证离散扩散语言模型微调中，来自去噪时间置信度监测器的诊断指标（top-1 argmax浓度）在PEFT训练场景下是否仍然有效。

Method: 在816个LoRA/PEFT配置（涵盖3个DLM系列）中测试top-1 argmax浓度作为崩溃预警的有效性；同时评估最大LoRA梯度范数作为替代信号，并在LLaDA系列上使用训练优化的阈值进行验证。

Result: top-1浓度预警在所有配置中均触发，但实际崩溃率为0/816，精度为零；最大LoRA梯度范数在LLaDA系列上对top-decile高损失配置达到0.68精度和0.79 F1。

Conclusion: top-1浓度不适合作为PEFT崩溃警报，应改用最大梯度早期监测并按DLM系列校准阈值；该结果仅限于短视界DLM-LoRA检测场景，非通用崩溃检测器。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language model (DLM) fine-tuning inherits inexpensive diagnostics from denoising-time confidence monitors, but their PEFT-training meaning is untested. We test top-1 argmax concentration as a collapse warning. Across 816 LoRA/PEFT configurations from three DLM families, the warning fires for every configuration while logs record 0/816 actual collapses at the 200 step horizon, giving zero precision. The cause is pre-equilibrium saturation: top-1 concentration is already high before optimization and quickly becomes insensitive to final training stability. We then evaluate max LoRA gradient norm, a parameter-side signal that samples gradient routing rather than token concentration. On a pooled held-out LLaDA-family split, a train-optimized threshold identifies top-decile final-loss configurations with precision 0.68 and F1=0.79, above the all-positive top-1 baseline even at the lower split-bootstrap confidence bound. Autoregressive controls and cross-family threshold failures bound the result to short-horizon DLM-LoRA inspection rather than a universal collapse detector. Workflow: drop top-1 as a PEFT alarm, log max-gradient early in training, and calibrate thresholds per DLM family before routing runs for inspection.

</details>


### [41] [Holistic Data Scheduler for LLM Pre-training via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24133)
*Chenhao Dang,Jing Ma,Mingjie Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HDS（整体数据调度器）的在线数据混合框架，通过将数据调度问题建模为连续控制空间中的强化学习问题，并利用SAC算法及多目标奖励函数，在LLM预训练中实现自适应数据混合。在The Pile基准上，HDS以比次优方法少44%的训练轮次达到相同的验证困惑度，并在MMLU 0-shot任务上提升7.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线数据混合方法仅依赖单一优化视角，无法满足LLM预训练中需要从多个维度考虑动态数据组成的需求。

Method: 将数据调度问题建模为连续控制空间中的强化学习问题，采用SAC算法进行策略探索，并设计了一个多目标整体奖励函数，融合了数据驱动（质量）、损失驱动（跨领域影响）和模型驱动（权重组范数）三个视角。

Result: 在The Pile基准上，HDS以比次优方法少44%的训练迭代达到相同验证困惑度；在MMLU 0-shot任务上提升7.2%，并在其他基准上取得一致提升。

Conclusion: HDS通过多维度优化视角和强化学习框架，有效提升了LLM预训练的数据混合效率和最终模型能力。

Abstract: The composition of training data, governed by the diversity of sources and their mixing strategy, is a cornerstone of Large Language Model (LLM) pre-training. Online Data Mixing (ODM), the technique of adaptively adjusting data mixtures during training, has emerged as a promising direction to improve efficiency. However, existing methods are constrained by their reliance on a singular optimization perspective, which fundamentally overlooks the need for complex LLM pre-training to consider the dynamic data composition from multiple dimensions. To overcome this limitation, we introduce the Holistic Data Scheduler (HDS), a novel online data mixing framework. HDS formulates the data scheduling challenge as a reinforcement learning problem in a continuous control space and leverages the Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm for its stability and sample efficiency in exploring the high-dimensional policy space. At the core of HDS lies a novel multi-objective, holistic reward function that integrates three critical perspectives: a data-driven reward for quality, a loss-driven reward capturing inter-domain influence, and a model-driven reward based on weight norms. To validate our design and determine its optimal configuration, we conducted systematic experiments on LLMs of various sizes. On The Pile benchmark, HDS reaches the final validation perplexity of the next best method with 44% fewer training iterations. Furthermore, it achieves a 7.2% improvement on the MMLU 0-shot task along with consistent gains on other benchmarks, showcasing its ability to enhance both training efficiency and final model capability.

</details>


### [42] [A Time-Reparameterized Cumulative Intensity Extrapolation Sampler for Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24140)
*Feiyang Fu,Hehe Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时间重参数化累积强度外推（TR-CIE）采样器，通过时间重参数化和累积强度外推更新规则，在有限函数评估次数下提升离散流匹配的采样质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散流匹配（DFM）在离散状态空间的生成建模中具有优势，但在有限函数评估次数（NFE）下的高效采样方法研究不足，现有τ-leaping采样器效率有待提升。

Method: TR-CIE包含两个组件：(1) 基于噪声调度的时序重参数化，重新缩放时间网格，吸收调度相关增长项并缓解终端采样阶段的刚性；(2) 累积强度外推更新规则，缓存上一步模型输出作为历史项，在非均匀时间网格上改进步进累积强度的近似。

Result: 在合成任务、文本生成和文生图基准测试上，TR-CIE在有限NFE下显著提升了采样质量，且每步仅需一次NFE，未引入额外模型评估。

Conclusion: TR-CIE是首个系统性地针对有限NFE下DFM采样优化的方法，理论分析了累积强度的局部近似误差并建立了收敛性结果。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching (DFM) provides a principled framework for generative modeling on discrete state spaces via continuous-time Markov chain dynamics. In practice, sampling for DFM commonly employs discretizations such as $τ$-leaping, yet efficient sampling methods under a limited number of function evaluations (NFE) remain less studied. To address this gap, we propose the Time-Reparameterized Cumulative Intensity Extrapolation (TR-CIE) sampler, which aims to improve sampling quality when function evaluations are restricted. TR-CIE consists of two components. First, a schedule-based time reparameterization rescales the time grid according to the noise schedule. Under standard factorized DFM rate parameterizations, this transformation of variables absorbs the schedule-dependent growth term and mitigates stiffness near the terminal sampling stage. Second, we introduce a cumulative-intensity extrapolation updating rule. By reusing cached model outputs from the previous step as a history term, this improves the approximation of stepwise cumulative intensities on the resulting non-uniform time grid. We provide a theoretical analysis that bounds the local approximation error of cumulative intensities and establishes convergence results. The resulting sampler requires one NFE per step and introduces no additional model evaluations compared to the standard $τ$-leaping sampler. Extensive experiments on synthetic tasks, text generation, and text-to-image benchmarks demonstrate that our method improves sampling quality under limited NFE.

</details>


### [43] [AsyncOPD: How Stale Can On-Policy Distillation Be?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24143)
*Wonjun Kang,Kevin Galim,Seunghyuk Oh,Minjun Kang,Sanghyun Park,Donghoon Kim,Minjae Lee,Minseo Kim,Rishabh Tiwari,Yuchen Zeng,Hyung Il Koo,Kangwook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了异步在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中的陈旧性问题，发现KL方向影响陈旧数据的鲁棒性（前向KL更鲁棒，反向KL更脆弱），并提出AsyncOPD训练框架，通过多采样蒙特卡洛估计器等方案，在保持精度的同时实现1.6x-3.8x的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在LLM后训练中日益重要，但其面临与强化学习类似的在线策略系统瓶颈——推理工作负载中，rollout生成可能主导训练时间。异步训练虽能缓解该瓶颈，但会引入陈旧策略数据。尽管异步RL中陈旧数据已被研究，其在OPD中的影响尚缺乏探索。

Method: 1) 系统分析KL方向对陈旧数据鲁棒性的影响；2) 评估异步RL稳定化方法对OPD陈旧性的缓解效果；3) 分析有限教师分数缓存带来的偏差-方差权衡，提出多采样蒙特卡洛（MC）估计器；4) 构建完全异步的OPD训练流水线AsyncOPD。

Result: 1) 教师加权前向KL对陈旧rollout更鲁棒，学生加权反向KL则脆弱；2) 异步RL稳定方法对反向KL无效，更优的OPD替代方案是推理时在当前学生下重计算反向KL信号；3) 多采样MC能保持MC可修正性并降低单样本方差；4) AsyncOPD在严格同步训练基础上实现1.6x-3.8x吞吐量提升，且精度相当。

Conclusion: 异步OPD中KL方向选择至关重要，前向KL天然抗陈旧性，反向KL需额外处理；简单重计算策略优于复杂异步RL方法；提出的AsyncOPD框架可有效提升训练效率而不牺牲模型质量。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts guided by teacher feedback and is becoming increasingly important for large language model (LLM) post-training. Like reinforcement learning (RL), however, OPD faces an on-policy systems bottleneck, as rollouts can dominate training time for reasoning workloads. Asynchronous training pipelines can alleviate this bottleneck by decoupling rollout generation from learner updates, but doing so introduces stale-policy data. While prior work has studied stale data in asynchronous RL, its effects in OPD remain underexplored. We present the first systematic study of staleness in asynchronous OPD, focusing on a practical setting where teacher feedback is implemented through local KL losses and full-vocabulary teacher logits are too expensive to store or transfer, necessitating finite teacher-score caches. We first show that KL direction changes the stale-data problem: teacher-weighted forward KL is more robust to stale rollouts, whereas student-weighted reverse KL is vulnerable. Second, for this vulnerable reverse-KL case, we study whether methods designed to stabilize asynchronous RL can mitigate OPD staleness. In our experiments, they do not improve over a simpler OPD-specific surrogate: recomputing the reverse-KL signal under the current student at learner time. Third, we analyze how finite teacher-score caches create a bias-variance tradeoff for sparse and sampled reverse-KL OPD estimators. This motivates multi-sample Monte Carlo (MC), which preserves MC correctability while reducing one-sample variance. Finally, we present and open-source AsyncOPD, a fully asynchronous OPD training pipeline built from these estimator choices. Experiments show that AsyncOPD improves training throughput by $1.6\times$ to $3.8\times$ over strict synchronous training while reaching comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [44] [Lightweight Transformer Models for On-Device Fault Detection: A Benchmark Study on Resource-Constrained Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24173)
*Disha Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对资源受限硬件上的设备故障检测，系统比较了传统机器学习方法（随机森林、XGBoost、SVM、逻辑回归）与轻量级Transformer架构（DistilBERT、TinyBERT-6L、TinyBERT-4L、MobileBERT）在三个公开数据集上的性能。结果表明：在数据分离良好的场景下，轻量级Transformer与传统方法F1分数相当（87.8%），但模型体积大100倍、延迟高9000倍；TinyBERT-4L是最部署友好的Transformer（55MB, 18ms）；INT8量化减少25%体积且保持86.9% F1；自适应推理管线（97.9%由量化轻量模型处理）达到87.6% F1和19.5ms平均延迟。在严重不平衡数据集上，所有方法均表现不佳，揭示了当前方法在极端类别不平衡故障检测中的根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限的硬件上部署机器学习模型进行实时设备故障检测时，需要在准确性、延迟和模型大小之间进行权衡，但目前缺乏对传统ML方法与轻量级Transformer在这一场景下系统性的基准比较研究。

Method: 在NASA C-MAPSS、SECOM、UCI AI4I 2020三个公开数据集上，对四种传统ML方法（随机森林、XGBoost、SVM、逻辑回归）和四种轻量级Transformer（DistilBERT、TinyBERT-6L、TinyBERT-4L、MobileBERT）进行二分类故障检测对比，评估指标包括F1分数、AUC、模型大小和CPU推理延迟，并进一步评估了INT8动态量化和两阶段自适应推理管线。

Result: 在C-MAPSS数据集上，轻量级Transformer达到87.8% F1，与传统ML相当，但模型体积大100倍、延迟高9000倍；TinyBERT-4L为最部署友好的Transformer（55MB, 18ms）；INT8量化减少25%体积，保持86.9% F1；自适应管线（97.9%预测由量化轻量模型处理）达到87.6% F1和19.5ms平均延迟。在SECOM和UCI-PM等严重不平衡数据集上，所有方法均表现不佳。

Conclusion: 在资源受限的故障检测场景中，传统ML方法在效率上显著优于轻量级Transformer；自适应推理管线可有效平衡性能与效率；INT8量化是有效的模型压缩手段；极端类别不平衡仍是当前故障检测方法面临的根本性挑战。

Abstract: On-device fault detection enables real-time diagnostics without cloud dependency, but deploying machine learning models on resource-constrained hardware demands careful tradeoffs between accuracy, latency, and model size. We present a benchmark comparing traditional ML methods (Random Forest, XGBoost, SVM, Logistic Regression) against lightweight transformer architectures (DistilBERT, TinyBERT-6L, TinyBERT-4L, MobileBERT) for binary fault detection across three public datasets: NASA C-MAPSS turbofan degradation, SECOM semiconductor manufacturing, and UCI AI4I 2020 predictive maintenance. We evaluate classification performance (F1-score, AUC), model size, and CPU inference latency, and further assess INT8 dynamic quantization and a two-stage adaptive inference pipeline. Our results reveal that on well-separated sensor data (C-MAPSS), lightweight transformers match traditional ML at 87.8% F1 but at 100x the model size and 9000x the latency. TinyBERT-4L emerges as the most deployment-friendly transformer at 55 MB and 18 ms CPU latency. INT8 quantization reduces size by 25% while preserving 86.9% F1. Our adaptive pipeline, routing 97.9% of predictions through a quantized triage model and only 2.1% to a larger expert, achieves 87.6% F1 at 19.5 ms average latency. On severely imbalanced datasets (SECOM, UCI-PM), both traditional and transformer methods struggle significantly, highlighting fundamental limitations of current approaches for extreme class imbalance in fault detection. All code is publicly available.

</details>


### [45] [Project Ariadne: Prompt-Conditioned Route Generation for Synthesis Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24184)
*Anton Morgunov,Victor S. Batista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Ariadne是一个解码器-only路线生成器，将逆合成路线生成建模为提示-补全序列生成，支持通过提示字段灵活施加深度约束和所需原料约束，在多项基准上取得竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆合成规划方法要么依赖单步反应模型的迭代搜索（需专用算法），要么是直接路线生成但不同规划规格需训练不同模型，缺乏统一灵活的生成框架。

Method: 提出Ariadne，一个解码器-only的路线生成器，将目标分子、可选约束条件和合成路线统一编码为提示-补全序列（prompt-completion sequence），通过单个24层Transformer checkpoint处理不同约束条件下的路线生成。

Result: 在RetroCast/PaRoutes mkt-cnv-160基准上，添加深度约束提示使Solv-0提升13.7点，添加所需原料约束提示使Solv-0提升31.2点；在所需原料约束的Top-10和Solv-0上超越DESP双向搜索规划器（24 GPU-min vs 6.8 GPU-hours）；在标准重建任务中与DMS Explorer XL相当，推理时间约为其一半。

Conclusion: 将序列生成从专用逆合成模型扩展为提示条件化的结构路线生成，但Tier-1-3路线检查器仍是模型实际应用的主要瓶颈。

Abstract: Retrosynthetic planning seeks to connect a target molecule to commercially available starting materials through a multistep route. Classical planners construct such routes by iteratively applying single-step reaction models within a search procedure; constrained variants often require specialized algorithms or architectural changes. Direct route generation reframes retrosynthesis as sequence generation, but existing direct-generation methods still train separate models for different planning specifications. We introduce Ariadne, a decoder-only route generator that represents the target, optional constraints, and route in one prompt-completion sequence. On the RetroCast/PaRoutes mkt-cnv-160 benchmark family, one 24-layer checkpoint follows route-depth and required-starting-material prompts: adding the corresponding prompt fields raises Solv-0 by 13.7 points for depth constraints and 31.2 points for required-leaf constraints. Ariadne also improves over DESP, a bidirectional search planner, on required-leaf Top-10 and Solv-0 in 24 GPU-minutes versus 6.8 GPU-hours. On standard reconstruction, Ariadne is comparable to DMS Explorer XL at about half the reported inference time. Across additional target-only benchmarks, Ariadne's clearest gains are on route-holdout reconstruction, whereas AiZynthFinder MCTS remains stronger on several Solv-0 comparisons. These results extend sequence generation from specialist retrosynthesis models to prompt-conditioned structural route generation. We release the codebase and training scripts to support further work, but do not introduce Tier-1--3 route checkers; those remain the main bottleneck before models of this kind can become useful to experimental chemists.

</details>

Abstract: In recent years, the Internet of Things (IoT) paradigm has been shifting toward batteryless, energy-harvesting architectures. Sustaining reliable operation in these systems requires intelligent management of highly volatile stored energy. As edge applications grow in complexity, traditional energy-aware schedulers struggle with unpredictable workloads due to their reliance on static execution thresholds or pre-measured, hardware-specific task profiles. To overcome this, we propose two novel, hardware-agnostic dynamic scheduling strategies treating applications as a "black box," requiring no prior energy information: a model-free Reinforcement Learning (RL) agent and an on-the-fly Approximated Prediction (AP) method. We evaluate these methods against an adaptive task rate approach (AsTAR) and optimized static thresholds using a custom-built, physically accurate simulation framework driven by real-world solar data and dynamic LoRa transmission profiles. Rather than claiming universal superiority, our analysis exposes the distinct operational trade-offs of each method: the AP approach delivers lightweight, near-oracle task throughput; the RL agent provides tunable survival-execution balancing; and AsTAR excels at execution pacing across long energy gaps. Finally, we demonstrate that while these advanced strategies provide critical resilience for severely constrained systems with small capacitors, devices with larger energy buffers can efficiently rely on simpler, less computationally expensive static policies.

</details>

Method: 提出H-Res（层次化残差引导），将适应问题形式化为激活流形上的控制问题，学习一个状态依赖向量场，引导令牌轨迹进入任务特定的吸引子盆地。该方法在不改变Transformer全局均衡或扩展序列长度的情况下，调制有效能量景观。

Result: 理论证明H-Res保留了基础模型的注意力熵并促进神经坍缩。实验表明，在联想检索任务上，流形引导方法比全局权重修改提升26%，并且消除了基于提示方法的计算开销，可有效扩展到结构化领域。

Conclusion: H-Res通过层次化残差引导机制，在不修改权重或不增加序列长度的前提下，有效解决了大模型适应新任务时的可塑性-稳定性困境，为冻结模型的高效适应提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Transformer models function as Dense Associative Memories (DAMs), retrieving knowledge via high-dimensional attractor dynamics driven by the self-attention mechanism \citep{ramsauer2020hopfield, wu2024attention}. However, adapting these frozen memory systems to new tasks presents a fundamental ``Plasticity-Stability'' dilemma. Current methods either risk catastrophic interference by modifying synaptic weights directly (e.g., LoRA) \citep{hu2021lora} or degrade associative capacity by clogging the retrieval buffer with static prompt tokens (e.g., VPT) \citep{jia2022vpt}. In this work, we propose \textbf{H-Res} (Hierarchical Residual Steering), a mechanism that modulates the effective energy landscape of the Transformer without altering its global equilibrium or expanding its sequence length. By formulating adaptation as a control problem on the activation manifold \citep{chen2018neuralode}, H-Res learns a state-dependent vector field that steers token trajectories into task-specific basins of attraction. We formally prove that H-Res preserves the attention entropy of the foundation model and facilitates Neural Collapse \citep{papyan2020prevalence}. Empirically, Manifold Steering outperforms global weight modification by 26\% on associative retrieval tasks and eliminates the computational overhead of prompt-based methods, scaling effectively to structured domains \citep{zha2023vtab}.

</details>


### [48] [Natural Identifiers for Privacy and Data Audits in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24408)
*Lorenzo Rossi,Bartłomiej Marek,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自然标识符（NIDs）——即训练数据中天然存在的结构化随机字符串（如加密哈希、短链接），用于解决LLM隐私审计中依赖人工插入金丝雀数据或需要私有非成员保留数据集的局限性，实现了无需重新训练的后验差分隐私审计和数据集推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM隐私审计方法存在两大瓶颈：一是差分隐私审计需要训练时插入特制金丝雀数据，无法审计已训练模型；二是数据集推理需要同分布私有非成员保留数据集，而实际中这类数据集难以获取。这些限制阻碍了可扩展的后验审计。

Method: 利用LLM训练数据中天然存在的结构化随机字符串（NIDs，如加密哈希、短链接），其格式允许生成无限同分布随机字符串，作为替代金丝雀数据用于差分隐私审计，同时作为同分布保留数据用于数据集推理。

Result: 实验证明，使用NIDs可以在无需重新训练的情况下实现后验差分隐私审计，并且对包含NIDs的怀疑数据集，无需私有非成员保留数据集即可进行数据集推理。

Conclusion: NIDs为LLM隐私审计提供了一种实用且可扩展的解决方案，克服了现有方法对人工构造数据和私有保留数据集的依赖，使得对已训练模型的后验审计成为可能。

Abstract: Assessing the privacy of large language models (LLMs) presents significant challenges. In particular, most existing methods for auditing differential privacy require the insertion of specially crafted canary data during training, making them impractical for auditing already-trained models without costly retraining. Additionally, dataset inference, which audits whether a suspect dataset was used to train a model, is infeasible without access to a private non-member held-out dataset. Yet, such held-out datasets are often unavailable or difficult to construct for real-world cases since they have to be from the same distribution (IID) as the suspect data. These limitations severely hinder the ability to conduct scalable, post-hoc audits. To enable such audits, this work introduces natural identifiers (NIDs) as a novel solution to the above-mentioned challenges. NIDs are structured random strings, such as cryptographic hashes and shortened URLs, naturally occurring in common LLM training datasets. Their format enables the generation of unlimited additional random strings from the same distribution, which can act as alternative canaries for audits and as same-distribution held-out data for dataset inference. Our evaluation highlights that indeed, using NIDs, we can facilitate post-hoc differential privacy auditing without any retraining and enable dataset inference for any suspect dataset containing NIDs without the need for a private non-member held-out dataset.

</details>


### [49] [Data Augmentation: A Fourier Analysis Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24418)
*Behrooz Tahmasebi,Melanie Weber,Stefanie Jegelka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过傅里叶分析和有限群表示论，建立了一个通用框架来研究部分数据增强是否能达到与完全数据增强相同的统计收益。结果表明，对于一类经典学习问题，基于随机采样子集的部分增强在最小化最优率上与完全增强一致，近似误差随子集增大而消失；但同时证明了精确不变性必须通过全群平均实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据增强虽能利用对称性提升学习性能，但当群规模较大时，完整的数据增强计算成本过高。作者试图回答一个根本性问题：部分数据增强能否在泛化性能和样本复杂度上达到与完全增强相同的统计收益？

Method: 利用傅里叶分析和有限群表示论，建立理论框架分析部分数据增强的统计性质；通过随机采样子集的方式进行部分增强，并与完全增强进行最小化最优率对比。

Result: 部分数据增强（随机采样子集）在最小化最优率上与完全增强一致，近似误差随子集规模增大而消失；但精确不变性必须通过全群平均才能实现，任何严格子集在假设空间足够表达时都无法达到。

Conclusion: 部分数据增强可以在计算可行的情况下保留完全增强的统计收益，为使用计算可扩展方法实现统计最优的对称性学习提供了理论依据；同时揭示了精确与近似对称性强制之间的基本界限。

Abstract: Data augmentation is a simple and model-agnostic approach for exploiting known invariances in learning problems. Given a group acting on the input space, one augments the training set with transformed copies of each sample. Because it exploits symmetries without modifying the underlying learning algorithm, data augmentation can be applied broadly across learning methods. However, this universality comes at a computational cost: when the group is large, full group-sized augmentation quickly becomes computationally infeasible. This raises a fundamental question: Can partial data augmentation achieve the same statistical benefits as full augmentation in terms of generalization and sample complexity? We develop a general framework for investigating this question using Fourier analysis and the representation theory of finite groups. We show that, for a broad class of classical learning problems, partial data augmentation based on a randomly sampled subset of group elements achieves the same minimax rates as full augmentation, up to an approximation error that vanishes as the subset size increases. Our results provide a theoretical explanation for why partial augmentation can retain the statistical benefits of full augmentation despite enforcing symmetry only approximately, and shed light on a recently raised question in learning with symmetries: whether statistically optimal learning under general group invariances can be achieved using computationally scalable methods. Moreover, we prove a complementary impossibility result: enforcing exact invariance via data augmentation requires averaging over the entire group, and cannot be achieved by any strict subset when the hypothesis space is sufficiently expressive. Together, these results provide a unified perspective on full and partial data augmentation, as well as exact and approximate symmetry enforcement.

</details>


### [50] [An LLM-based Two-Stage Transformer Framework for Cross-Domain Bearing Fault Diagnosis with Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24459)
*Jinghan Wang,Feng Cheng,Wentao Wu,Hang Li,Gaoliang Peng,Tianchen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种知识引导的两阶段迁移学习框架，采用轻量级GPT-2风格Transformer进行轴承故障诊断，在仅有10%标签数据的情况下达到92.61%平均准确率，超越现有方法17.24个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业环境下数据集异构性、工况变化和有限标签数据同时存在时，现有方法孤立处理这些问题且依赖隐式特征对齐，效果受限。

Method: 采用轻量级GPT-2风格Transformer（因果自注意力）进行层次化特征提取；多源预训练编码器权重和故障原型嵌入作为知识载体；多源学习、原型知识调制和分类学自适应分类三方面应对双重漂移挑战。

Result: 在四个真实数据集上，仅用10%标签目标数据达到92.61%平均准确率，优于现有方法17.24个百分点。

Conclusion: 该框架为工业4.0中经济高效的预测性维护提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Bearing fault diagnosis faces critical challenges when dataset heterogeneity, operating condition variations, and limited labeled data occur simultaneously in industrial environments. Existing approaches address these issues in isolation and rely on implicit feature alignment, limiting effectiveness under concurrent challenges. This paper proposes a knowledge-guided two-stage transfer learning framework that employs a lightweight GPT-2-style Transformer with causal self-attention for hierarchical feature extraction from vibration signals, establishing explicit pathways where pre-trained encoder weights and fault prototype embeddings serve as knowledge carriers from multi-source pre-training to target adaptation. The framework addresses the dual-shift challenge through multi-source learning for generalizable representations, prototype-based knowledge modulation for target adaptation, and taxonomy-adaptive classification for seamless transfer across heterogeneous fault categories. Experimental validation on four real-world datasets demonstrates 92.61% average accuracy with only 10% labeled target data, outperforming state-of-the-art methods by 17.24 percentage points, establishing a practical pathway toward cost-effective predictive maintenance in Industry 4.0 applications.

</details>


### [51] [A Fair Evaluation of Graph Foundation Models for Node Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24509)
*Oleg Platonov,Gleb Bazhenov,Dmitry Eremeev,Liudmila Prokhorenkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对9种面向节点属性预测的图基础模型（GFMs）进行了公平且严格的重新评估，发现只有基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的最新GFMs在预测性能上超越调优后的GNN，但推理成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图基础模型（GFMs）在节点属性预测任务上受到广泛关注，但该领域缺乏统一的评估标准，不同工作采用差异化的评估方式，导致GFMs之间及与其他模型之间无法进行可靠比较。

Method: 对9种近期提出的用于节点属性预测的GFMs进行公平且严格的重新评估，并将其与强图神经网络（GNN）基线进行对比。

Result: 只有基于Prior-data Fitted Networks范式的最新GFMs在预测性能上优于调优后的GNN，但推理成本更高。

Conclusion: 当前多数GFMs在节点属性预测任务上并未显著超越well-tuned的GNN；该领域需要统一的评估标准以促进可靠比较。

Abstract: Due to the wide use of graph-structured data in different fields of industry and science, the development of Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) has recently attracted a lot of attention. While many different types of models are called GFMs, particular interest has been paid to GFMs designed for node property prediction tasks, which is one of the most popular settings in Graph ML with lots of real-world applications from fraud detection in financial and social networks to recommendation systems for e-commerce and user-generated content platforms. While a number of GFMs for this task have been recently proposed, the field has not converged to a unified evaluation setting, and different works evaluate their models in widely different ways, preventing reliable comparison of GFMs with each other and with other types of models. In this work, we conduct a fair and rigorous reevaluation of 9 recent GFMs for node property prediction, comparing them to strong Graph Neural Network (GNN) baselines. We find that, among these GFMs, only the most recent ones based on the Prior-data Fitted Networks paradigm outperform well-tuned GNNs in predictive performance, although at a higher inference cost.

</details>


### [52] [Reasoning as Attractor Dynamics: Latent Memory Retrieval via Gibbs-Weighted Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24543)
*Kanishk Awadhiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从集体计算视角重新审视大语言模型，将其视为高维稠密联想记忆网络，数学推理的正确链对应平坦吸引子盆地（低能量、宽最小值），而幻觉对应尖锐不稳定局部极小值。受此启发，提出基于吉布斯测度与谱熵的检索机制，通过采样多条推理路径并按能量倒数加权，模拟联想记忆的平衡分布，使系统"松弛"到稳健解。该方法在GSM8K上提升Phi-3.5性能5.38%（84.7%→90.1%），表明推理应建模为动态沉降到吸引子盆地的过程，而非贪婪自回归解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将LLM视为自回归生成模型，但作者从集体计算角度重新解读，认为LLM本质上是高维稠密联想记忆网络。数学推理中的正确推理链对应能量景观中深宽的吸引子盆地（平坦最小值），而幻觉对应尖锐不稳定的局部极小值。基于这一几何直觉，作者希望利用能量景观结构来改进推理质量，将推理过程建模为系统动态沉降到吸引子盆地的过程。

Method: 提出基于吉布斯测度与轨迹谱熵的检索机制：(1) 对同一问题采样多条推理路径；(2) 计算每条路径的能量（基于谱熵）；(3) 按玻尔兹曼分布加权（P ∝ e^{-βE}），即能量越低权重越高；(4) 通过加权聚合近似联想记忆的平衡分布，使系统"松弛"到鲁棒解。

Result: 在GSM8K数学推理数据集上，该方法将Microsoft Phi-3.5的性能从84.7%提升至90.1%，绝对提升5.38个百分点。

Conclusion: 推理不应被建模为贪婪的逐词预测，而应理解为系统在能量景观中动态沉降到吸引子盆地的过程。物理启发的松弛机制（能量加权采样）能有效缓解幻觉，提升推理鲁棒性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are traditionally viewed as autoregressive generators. However, from the perspective of collective computation, they function as high-dimensional Dense Associative Memories that store complex reasoning patterns as latent attractors. In this work, we investigate the energy landscape of mathematical reasoning. We posit that correct reasoning chains correspond to deep, wide attractor basins ("flat minima") in the model's output distribution, whereas hallucinations manifest as sharp, unstable local minima. To exploit this geometry, we introduce a retrieval mechanism based on a Gibbs measure of the trajectory's spectral entropy. By sampling multiple reasoning paths and weighting them by their inverse energy ($P \propto e^{-βE}$), we approximate the equilibrium distribution of the associative memory, effectively ``relaxing'' the system into a robust solution. Empirically, this physics-inspired mechanism improves Microsoft Phi-3.5 performance on GSM8K by 5.38\% (84.7\% $\to$ 90.1\%), demonstrating that inference is better modeled as a dynamic settling process into an attractor basin rather than greedy next-token prediction.

</details>


### [53] [QC-SMOTE: Quality-Controlled SMOTE for Imbalanced Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24625)
*Parth Upman,Shreyank N Gowda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出QC-SMOTE，一种质量控制的过采样框架，通过复合邻域可信度评分评估少数类样本可靠性，结合IPQ引导的最佳K策略和自适应生成机制，在30个不平衡数据集上取得了最优的AUC-ROC和Macro F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过采样方法（如SMOTE）在噪声或类重叠区域常生成低质量合成样本，影响分类性能，亟需一种质量感知的合成采样方法。

Method: 1) 复合邻域可信度评分：结合局部密度、安全级别和与多数类隔离度评估可靠性；2) IPQ引导的最佳K策略：评估中点纯度并必要时进行多数类清理；3) 自适应生成机制：根据重叠-不平衡状态调整插值范围和选择标准；4) 质量退化回退：低质量合成样本替换为原始多数类副本。

Result: 在30个不平衡数据集上采用重复分层交叉验证，QC-SMOTE在AUC-ROC和Macro F1上优于所有对比的过采样方法，在中度和严重不平衡场景下增益尤为明显。

Conclusion: 质量感知、几何自适应的合成采样对鲁棒的不平衡分类至关重要，QC-SMOTE通过质量控制和退化回退机制有效应对噪声和重叠区域问题。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a significant challenge in classification, where existing methods such as SMOTE often generate low-quality synthetic samples in regions with noise or class overlap. We propose QC-SMOTE, a quality-controlled oversampling framework that estimates minority sample reliability using a composite neighbourhood trustworthiness score combining local density, safe-level, and isolation from the majority class. Synthetic candidates are generated using an IPQ-guided best-of-K strategy that evaluates midpoint purity and, when required, majority clearance, with allocation guided by sample reliability and boundary informativeness. Generation behaviour adapts across overlap--imbalance regimes, adjusting interpolation range and selection criteria to match local data geometry. Low-quality synthetic samples are replaced with original minority duplicates when neighbourhood purity falls below an adaptive threshold, providing graceful degradation by reverting to duplication in severely noisy regions. Experiments on 30 imbalanced datasets using repeated stratified cross-validation show that QC-SMOTE achieves the strongest average AUC-ROC and Macro F1 among the compared oversampling methods, with particularly clear gains under moderate and severe imbalance. These results demonstrate the importance of quality-aware, geometry-adaptive synthetic sampling for robust imbalanced classification.

</details>


### [54] [FlowPipe: LLM-Enhanced Conditional Generative Flow Networks for Data Preparation Pipeline Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24679)
*Kunyu Ni,Lei Cao,Jie He,Xiaotong Zhang,Jianfeng Jin,Junyu Dong,Yanwei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowPipe是一个基于条件生成流网络（C-GFlowNets）的自动化数据准备流水线合成框架，通过轨迹平衡目标、FiLM深度语义调制和失败感知机制，在74个真实数据集上比SOTA方法平均提升11.96%准确率，训练收敛速度提升12.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA方法（Multi-DQN）存在三个关键局限：解耦的值估计器削弱了长时程信用分配、数据集上下文仅被弱注入策略、稀疏搜索空间中无效状态太多导致探索效率低下。因此需要一种统一框架来克服这些问题。

Method: 将流水线合成建模为有向无环图上的条件概率流生成问题。采用C-GFlowNets结合Trajectory Balance目标来连接终端验证奖励与早期决策；通过FiLM实现深度语义调制，将LLM导出的逻辑先验根据数据集语义条件化策略的内部激活；将失败感知纳入流目标以避免无效状态并集中搜索高潜力区域。

Result: 在两个基准套件（74个真实数据集）上的实验表明，FlowPipe比SOTA基线平均提升11.96%的准确率，训练收敛速度提升12.5倍。

Conclusion: FlowPipe通过统一的条件概率流生成框架，有效解决了自动化数据准备流水线合成中的组合搜索、长时程信用分配和无效状态探索问题，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Data preparation pipelines improve data quality in machine learning by transforming raw tables into learning-ready data through sequential cleaning and feature transformation operators. However, automatically constructing such pipelines is computationally difficult because operator sequences are combinatorial and end-to-end evaluation is expensive. Existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) Multi-DQN methods still face three key limitations: decoupled value estimators weaken long-horizon credit assignment, dataset context is only weakly injected into the policy, and exploration is inefficient in a sparse search space with many invalid states. To address these issues, we propose FlowPipe, a unified framework that formulates pipeline synthesis as conditional probabilistic flow generation over a directed acyclic graph. FlowPipe uses Conditional Generative Flow Networks (C-GFlowNets) with a Trajectory Balance objective to connect terminal validation rewards with early pipeline decisions. It further introduces Deep Semantic Modulation through Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), allowing LLM-derived logical priors to condition the policy's internal activations according to dataset semantics. In addition, FlowPipe incorporates failure awareness into the flow objective to avoid invalid states and concentrate search on high-potential regions. Experiments on two benchmark suites with 74 real-world datasets show that FlowPipe outperforms SOTA baselines, improving accuracy by 11.96% on average and achieving 12.5x faster training convergence. Source code is available at https://github.com/KunyuNi/FlowPipe.

</details>


### [55] [Grad Detect: Gradient-Based Hallucination Detection in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24790)
*Anand Kamat,Daniel Blake,Brent M. Werness*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Grad Detect方法，通过分析单次前向-反向传播中的逐层梯度模式来预测LLM的幻觉，发现最后五层包含97%以上的判别性梯度信号，在QA基准测试中优于基于置信度和采样的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生成输出时容易出现幻觉（hallucination），在高风险应用中需要可靠地检测这些幻觉。现有方法主要依赖输出层信号，无法获取模型内部的梯度结构信息。

Method: Grad Detect：在推理过程中通过单次前向-反向传播，分析模型各层的梯度模式来预测输出是否正确。方法利用了反向传播过程中产生的梯度信息，这些信息反映了模型内部对输出正确性的置信度。

Result: 在多个QA基准测试中，Grad Detect在幻觉检测和模型弃权预测任务上均优于基于置信度和采样的基线方法。通过覆盖11个模型的层消融实验，发现最后五层集中了超过97%的判别性梯度信号。

Conclusion: Grad Detect提供了一个统一框架，能够预测LLM可靠性的多个维度，兼具强预测性能和可解释性，揭示了模型失败发生的位置和原因。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across diverse tasks, yet they remain prone to generating hallucinations. Detecting these hallucinations is critical for deploying LLMs reliably in high-stakes applications. We present Grad Detect, a gradient-based approach for predicting hallucinations by analyzing layer-wise gradient patterns from a single forward-backward pass during inference. Our method shows that the internal gradient structure of a model carries rich information about the correctness of its output. This information is not accessible through output-level signals alone. We evaluate Grad Detect on several Q&A benchmarks across both hallucination detection and model abstention prediction, where it consistently outperforms confidence-based and sampling-based baselines. Through comprehensive layer ablation studies across all eleven models from four architectural families, we find that the final five layers concentrate over 97% of the discriminative gradient signal, enabling efficient deployment with minimal performance loss. Grad Detect provides a unified framework for predicting multiple dimensions of LLM reliability, offering strong predictive performance alongside interpretable insights into where and how model failures originate.

</details>


### [56] [Real vs. Complex Spectral Bases for Neural Operators: The Role of Green's Function Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24851)
*Jason Sulskis,Sathya Ravi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hartley神经算子（HNO），作为Fourier神经算子（FNO）的纯实数等价物，用离散Hartley变换替换FFT。通过理论分析和实验发现，对于自伴椭圆算子（如Poisson、双调和方程），HNO更优，因为其实数对称格林函数可被Hartley乘子精确对角化；而对于含相位的时间依赖算子（如波动、对流、Burgers、Navier-Stokes），FNO更优。热方程作为无相边界情况两者相当。结论是应将谱基与解算子的对称性匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FNO在复数Fourier域中参数化全局卷积，但对于实值PDE解，复数FFT通过共轭对称性引入了表示冗余。本文旨在消除这种冗余，引入纯实数的神经算子，并探究不同谱基对各类PDE算子的适用性。

Method: 将FNO中的FFT替换为纯实数离散Hartley变换（DHT），每个保留谱模式只学习一个实数乘子，无需复数运算。由于Hartley谱不被共轭对称性减半，在等宽度条件下HNO保留两倍于FNO的频率角，但每个频率用一个实权重替换FNO的一个复数对，因此两算子等参数。

Result: 发现了椭圆型算子与时间依赖算子之间的明确分界：自伴椭圆算子（Poisson、双调和）HNO更优，时间依赖含相算子（波动、对流、Burgers、Navier-Stokes）FNO更优且随相位含量增加优势增大，热方程（无相）为边界情况。该分界与算子相位含量单调相关，与格林函数理论匹配。

Conclusion: 不存在普适的优胜算子，应基于预测性规则选择：将谱基与解算子的对称性相匹配。自伴椭圆算子用实数Hartley基，含相位的时间依赖算子用复数Fourier基。

Abstract: Fourier Neural Operators (FNO) learn solution operators of partial differential equations by parameterizing global convolutions in the complex Fourier domain. For real-valued PDE solutions, the complex FFT carries representational redundancy through conjugate symmetry. We introduce the Hartley Neural Operator (HNO), the exact real-valued mirror of FNO: it replaces the FFT with the purely real Discrete Hartley Transform and learns a single real multiplier per retained spectral mode, with no complex arithmetic. Because the real Hartley spectrum is not halved by conjugate symmetry, HNO retains twice as many frequency corners as FNO but one real weight where FNO carries a complex pair, so the two operators are iso-parametric at equal width and differ only in spectral basis. Our central thesis is that the best basis is a property of the operator. Self-adjoint elliptic operators (Poisson, biharmonic) have real, symmetric Green's functions that the real Hartley multiplier diagonalizes exactly, and HNO is favored there. Time-dependent operators carry phase, from oscillation in the wave equation to transport in advection, Burgers, and Navier-Stokes, which a real diagonal multiplier cannot represent, so FNO is favored there, and increasingly so with the operator's phase content, leaving the phaseless heat equation as the borderline case. Training both operators identically and benchmarking across PDE classes, initial-condition families, and boundary conditions, we find an elliptic-versus-time-dependent split that is monotone in operator phase content and matches the Green's-function theory we develop. Rather than a universal winner, our findings give a predictive rule: match the spectral basis to the symmetry of the solution operator.

</details>

### [57] [Memory Layouts for GPU-Data Transfer Buffering in SPH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23891)
*Mladen Ivkovic,Abouzied M. A. Nasar,Tobias Weinzierl,Matthieu Schaller,Benedict D. Rogers,Georgios Fourtakas,Scott T. Kay*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 随着GPU计算速度提升超过主机-设备内存传输速度，内存传输时间已占总求解时间的更大比例。本文针对光滑粒子流体动力学求解器，提出了一种在主机端将传统数组结构体拆分为分裂数组结构体的内存布局策略，根据内核读写访问模式和属性类型将逻辑结构体分解为子结构体，从而将数据打包时间减少约20%-40%，GPU卸载总时间降低约12%-25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU计算速度的提升远超主机到设备内存传输速度的提升，导致内存传输时间在总求解时间中占比日益增大，亟需在GPU卸载前对内核输入输出数据进行紧凑格式压缩。

Method: 针对SPH求解器，提出将传统的数组结构体（AoS）拆分为分裂数组结构体（split AoS）的内存布局策略，即根据内核读写访问模式和属性类型将单个逻辑结构体分解为多个细粒度子结构体，以优化主机端粒子数据布局。

Result: 分裂后的数据结构可将数据打包到缓冲区及从缓冲区解包的时间减少约20%-40%，GPU卸载总时间降低约12%-25%。

Conclusion: 在主机端采用针对GPU卸载优化的分裂数组结构体布局策略，可有效缓解内存传输瓶颈，显著降低GPU卸载开销。

Abstract: The rise in GPU compute speed has outpaced improvements in host-to-device memory transfer speeds, despite the advent of shared-memory superchips. Consequently, memory transfer times now constitute an increasingly large fraction of total time-to-solution, compelling developers to compress GPU kernel input and output data into compact, minimal formats prior to GPU-offloading. This complements existing work on GPU- and compute-friendly data arrangements. We study a Smoothed Particle Hydrodynamics solver and propose memory layout strategies for host-side particle data that are particularly well-suited to GPU-offloading. Specifically, we advocate splitting classic array-of-struct data structures into a split array-of-struct arrangement, in which each logical struct decomposes into substructs determined by kernel read/write access patterns and attribute types. Splitting a monolithic particle struct into several bespoke, finer-grained structs can reduce the time required to pack data to and from buffers by ~20% - 40%, lowering total time spent on GPU-offloading by ~12% - 25%.

</details>


### [58] [An Efficient Construction of Completely Independent Spanning Trees in Dense Gaussian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23935)
*Zaid Hussain,Fawaz AlAzemi,Bader AlBdaiwi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在高斯网络中高效构建完全独立生成树（CISTs）的方法，通过将网络划分为集合并合理连接节点以构建第一棵CIST，再通过旋转得到第二棵CIST，其深度优于现有方法。相比现有方法，该方法在消息传递效率上至少提升了33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路由和广播的容错性对于通信网络的可靠性和鲁棒性至关重要，尤其是在易发生故障的环境中。构建完全独立生成树（CISTs）是提升大规模互连系统容错性、可靠性和通信效率的关键，但在高斯网络中构建CISTs仍是一个挑战。

Method: 将高斯网络划分为若干集合，通过合理的节点连接构建第一棵CIST；随后对第一棵CIST进行旋转操作得到第二棵CIST，该方法使得第二棵CIST的深度小于现有最优方法。

Result: 通过计算根节点到所有其他节点传递消息所需的平均最大步数进行评估，与现有方法相比，该构造方法在消息传递效率上至少提升了33%。

Conclusion: 所提出的CISTs构造方法在高斯网络中实现了更优的容错性和通信效率，显著优于现有技术。

Abstract: Fault tolerance in routing and broadcasting is a critical aspect in ensuring the reliability and robustness of communication networks, particularly in environments prone to failures. This work presents an efficient method for constructing Completely Independent Spanning Trees (CISTs) within dense Gaussian networks, providing improved fault tolerance, reliability, and communication efficiency in large-scale interconnection systems. To construct the CISTs in the Gaussian network, we partition the network into sets, and accordingly the nodes are connected properly to form the first CIST and then rotated to get the second CIST with less depth than the existing state-of-art. To evaluate the performance of the proposed construction, we calculated the average maximum number of steps required to deliver a message from the root node to all other nodes in the network. A comparison with existing approaches shows that our construction outperforms them, achieving an improvement of at least 33%

</details>


### [59] [The Serialized Bridge: Understanding and Recovering LLM Serving Performance under Blackwell GPU Confidential Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23969)
*Hang Yin,Kevin Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了机密GPU计算（GPU-CC）环境下LLM推理性能下降的主要瓶颈并非GPU计算本身，而是机密VM-GPU桥接导致的host/device数据传输序列化、高开销问题。在Blackwell平台上，非机密与机密计算间的性能差距可达13-27%，而通过调度优化可恢复57%-92%的性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管NVIDIA B300上BF16矩阵乘法在机密模式下几乎无性能损失（0.998x），但实际LLM推理服务在Intel TDX+GPU-CC组合下仍有13-27%的吞吐量损失，KV-cache恢复延迟甚至翻倍。作者旨在探究这一差距的根本原因。

Method: 在两个Blackwell平台（RTX Pro 6000和B300 HGX）上进行系统性分析，识别性能瓶颈的位置。通过细粒度基准测试发现GPU-CC将host/device数据传输转变为序列化、高设置成本的通道：安全拷贝无法获得CUDA流并发性、异步传输在运行时边界阻塞、小传输需支付固定开销。通过调度标志和worker-thread drain进行优化实验。

Result: 性能差距主要源于44倍慢的小型alloc-copy操作。调度标志可恢复57%的差距，在高并发场景下worker-thread drain可恢复高达92%的性能。该桥接模型同样解释了+131%的KV-restore惩罚和34倍的模型加载减速。

Conclusion: 机密VM-GPU桥接是性能损失的主导因素，而非GPU计算本身。Blackwell平台还改变了机密租用单元，作者验证了B300上机密多GPU NVSwitch租户（包括CVM内510 GB/s NVLink P2P和并发隔离租户），并指出了生产级机密AI平台尚需解决的fabric-attestation差距。

Abstract: GPU Confidential Computing (GPU-CC) now preserves GPU-local performance: on NVIDIA B300, BF16 matmul runs at 0.998x of non-confidential performance. Yet LLM serving under Intel TDX plus GPU-CC still loses 13-27% of throughput, and KV-cache restore latency can more than double. This paper studies that gap on two Blackwell platforms, RTX Pro 6000 and B300 HGX, and identifies its dominant cause: the confidential VM-GPU bridge, not GPU compute.
  We find that GPU-CC turns host/device movement into a serialized, high-setup-cost channel. Secure copies do not gain CUDA-stream concurrency within a context, asynchronous transfers block at the runtime boundary, and small crossings pay a fixed toll. This violates the assumptions of modern inference runtimes, where DMA is expected to be cheap, concurrent, and asynchronous. In vLLM dense decode, the gap closes around 44x-slower small alloc-and-copy operations; targeted patches reject alternative explanations. A scheduling flag recovers 57% of the gap, while a worker-thread drain recovers up to 92% in qualified high-concurrency runs. The same bridge model explains a +131% KV-restore penalty and a 34x model-load slowdown.
  Blackwell also changes the confidential tenancy unit. We qualify confidential multi-GPU NVSwitch tenants on B300, including 510 GB/s NVLink P2P inside a CVM and concurrent isolated tenants, and identify the remaining fabric-attestation gap for production confidential AI platforms.

</details>


### [60] [Hash Table Design for RDMA:Challenges and Opportunities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24073)
*Shuchen She,Haipeng Dai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于RDMA的远程哈希表设计，分析了其在IoT大数据场景下面临的三个核心挑战：网络访问次数增加、远程并发控制失效以及RDMA网卡资源限制，并讨论了优化方向与应对策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着IoT设备数量和感知数据量持续增长，哈希表的内存消耗越来越大。单机内存不足时，需将数据分布到其他节点；RDMA远程直接内存访问技术可用于构建内存解耦系统以扩展容量，但哈希表部署在RDMA远程内存上存在诸多问题，亟需系统性研究与优化。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，梳理现有的RDMA哈希表相关工作，提炼关键挑战，并讨论有前景的优化方向和应对策略。

Result: 识别出RDMA哈希表设计的三大核心问题：(1) 逻辑访问可能引发多次RDMA请求，碰撞处理进一步加剧；(2) 远程CPU被旁路，传统依赖远程线程的并发控制无法直接适用；(3) RDMA网卡资源（队列、缓存、内存注册、原子操作）有限，对哈希表结构施加新约束。

Conclusion: 本文旨在为IoT大数据场景下的远程哈希表设计提供参考，指出优化方向和技术策略，帮助应对RDMA环境下的性能与资源限制挑战。

Abstract: Hash tables complete the insertion, lookup, and deletion of a single key in constant time on average, and they are widely used in databases, key-value stores, and network systems. In the Internet of Things (IoT), the number of devices and the volume of sensed data keep growing, so the hash tables that store or index these data consume more and more memory. When a single server runs out of memory, the system can place part of the data in the memory of other nodes. One-sided operations in Remote Direct Memory Access (RDMA) let one machine read and write the memory of another machine directly, with low latency and high bandwidth, and are therefore widely used to build disaggregated memory systems. Deploying a hash table on RDMA-based remote memory exploits the memory of other nodes and thus relieves the capacity limit of a single server. However, this deployment raises three problems. First, one logical hash-table access may translate into one or more remote network accesses, and collision handling and probing further increase the number of RDMA requests. Second, because the remote CPU is bypassed, traditional concurrency control that relies on remote threads no longer applies directly. Third, the limited resources of RDMA network interface cards, such as queues, caches, memory registration, and atomic operations, impose new constraints on hash table structures. This paper focuses on hash table design for RDMA. We review existing work, distill the key challenges, and discuss promising optimization directions and coping strategies, aiming to provide a reference for designing remote hash tables in IoT big-data scenarios.

</details>


### [61] [Aquifer: Hierarchical Memory Pooling with CXL and RDMA for MicroVM Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24079)
*Junliang Hu,Huaicheng Li,Ming-Chang Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Aquifer是首个面向MicroVM无服务器计算的分层CXL+RDMA内存池系统，通过热感知快照格式、所有权一致性协议和拷贝式页面服务机制，实现2.2倍于Firecracker的端到端性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云集群中25-35%的DRAM因内存滞留而浪费，CXL和RDMA各自存在局限性（CXL延迟低但覆盖范围限于pod，RDMA覆盖集群但延迟高且有软件开销），而分层架构在MicroVM无服务器计算场景中尚未被探索，且快照恢复延迟是冷启动的主要瓶颈。

Method: 提出热感知快照格式（消除零页，热工作集存CXL池、冷页存RDMA池）；针对CXL 2.0多头设备缺乏硬件缓存一致性问题，设计基于所有权的软件一致性协议；采用拷贝式页面服务机制（热页在MicroVM恢复前预装，冷页通过RDMA异步按需调页）。

Result: 在模拟CXL+RDMA硬件上，端到端调用时间相比Firecracker加速2.2倍（几何均值），相比次优方案加速1.1倍。

Conclusion: 分层CXL+RDMA内存池结合热感知快照格式和优化服务机制，能有效缓解无服务器计算中内存滞留和冷启动延迟问题。

Abstract: Memory stranding wastes 25-35% of installed DRAM in production cloud clusters. Memory pooling over CXL and RDMA offers a remedy, but neither technology alone suffices: CXL provides low-latency, load/store-transparent access limited to a pod, while RDMA provides cluster-wide reach at higher latency with software overhead. A hierarchical architecture combining both tiers is the practical path forward, yet remains unexplored for MicroVM-based serverless computing, where snapshot restore latency is the dominant cold-start bottleneck.
  We present Aquifer, the first system to serve MicroVM snapshots from a hierarchical CXL+RDMA memory pool. A characterization of snapshot images reveals that the vast majority of pages are either zero or cold, enabling a hotness-based snapshot format that eliminates zero pages and places only the hot working set in the CXL pool while storing cold pages in the RDMA pool. Sharing these snapshots across hosts on CXL 2.0 multi-headed devices, which lack hardware cache coherence, requires Aquifer's ownership-based coherence protocol to ensure correctness. Finally, Aquifer uses a copy-based page serving mechanism pre-installs hot pages from CXL memory before MicroVM resume and demand-pages cold pages asynchronously from RDMA. On emulated CXL+RDMA hardware, Aquifer achieves a 2.2x geometric-mean speedup in end-to-end invocation time over Firecracker and 1.1x over the next best alternative.

</details>


### [62] [Semi-asynchronous Federated Learning in Flower: Framework Extension and Performance Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24230)
*Víctor Hidalgo-Izquierdo,Carmen Carrión,Blanca Caminero*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在Flower联邦学习框架中扩展实现了半异步联邦学习策略FedSaSync，通过部分同步机制在异构环境下减少客户端空闲时间，平衡收敛稳定性与系统效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统同步联邦学习在客户端异构和掉队者效应下效率低下，亟需一种能兼顾训练效率与收敛稳定性的新策略。

Method: 在Flower框架中扩展实现半异步联邦学习，提出FedSaSync策略，允许客户端部分同步，并评估其在异构环境中的表现。

Result: 相比完全同步基线，半异步策略在异构环境中显著减少了空闲时间，提升了系统鲁棒性和效率。

Conclusion: 半异步联邦学习能在边缘和分布式学习场景中有效平衡收敛稳定性与系统效率，更适合处理客户端异构性问题。

Abstract: This paper presents an extension of the Flower federated learning framework to support Semi-Asynchronous Federated Learning. The proposed approach adapts the traditional synchronous paradigm to better handle client heterogeneity and straggler effects. By introducing a semi-asynchronous training strategy, the system allows partial synchronization among clients while maintaining training efficiency and scalability. We implement and evaluate the proposed modification within Flower, instantiated as the FedSaSync strategy, demonstrating improved robustness and reduced idle time compared to fully synchronous baselines in heterogeneous environments. The results show that SAFL can balance convergence stability and system efficiency in heterogeneous environments typical of edge and distributed learning scenarios.

</details>


### [63] [Semantic Lock: Synchronization Based on the Analysis of the Operation Conflict Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24250)
*Denis Korotchenko,Vitaly Aksenov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于操作间冲突图的SemanticLock，可视为读写锁的泛化，并在两个应用中验证了其有效性：支持点查询和范围查询的数组数据结构，以及为ConcurrentHashMap增加长时运行操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的读写锁只支持读-写冲突，无法处理更复杂的操作间冲突关系（如写-写冲突、范围查询之间的冲突等），需要一种更通用的锁机制来支持更细粒度的并发控制。

Method: 基于操作间的冲突图（conflict graph）来定义锁的兼容性，将读写锁泛化为更通用的语义锁，其中写操作与所有其他操作存在冲突，同时支持更多样化的操作类型及其冲突关系。

Result: 在两种应用中验证了有效性：1）设计了支持点查询和不同范围查询的数组数据结构；2）为ConcurrentHashMap增加了额外的长时运行操作，提升了并发性能。

Conclusion: SemanticLock作为一种基于冲突图的通用锁机制，能够有效支持比传统读写锁更丰富的操作类型和冲突关系，在实际并发数据结构中有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper presents a new lock, SemanticLock, based on the conflict graph between operations. We can consider it a generalization of a read-write lock where conflicts exist between write operations and all other operations. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our lock in two applications. In the first, we design a toy data structure: an array supporting point queries and different range queries. In the second, potentially of greater interest, we augment an existing concurrent data structure, ConcurrentHashMap, with additional long-running operations.

</details>


### [64] [BiJuTy: An Interactive HPC-Aware Big Data Cluster Lifecycle Manager and Performance Assessment Utility for JupyterHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24412)
*Apurv Deepak Kulkarni,Jan Frenzel,Siavash Ghiasvand*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: BiJuTy 是一个基于 Jupyter 生态的交互式解决方案，旨在降低在 HPC 系统上执行大数据工作流的门槛，提供从集群配置、生命周期管理到性能评估的一站式可视化操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大数据处理需求激增，HPC 系统的使用门槛高，尤其是对初学者而言，缺乏易用的交互工具来管理集群和执行大数据工作流。

Method: 在 Jupyter Notebook 环境中构建交互式界面，提供集群配置引导、多集群管理、性能指标采集与可视化，支持迭代优化工作流。

Result: 通过示例展示，用户仅需几次点击即可完成大数据处理生命周期的迭代执行与优化，显著简化了性能调优流程。

Conclusion: BiJuTy 降低了 HPC 上大数据工作流的操作复杂度，促进了大数据计算与高性能计算之间的无缝交互与可用性提升。

Abstract: The increasing demand for data processing has created a pressing need for access to high-performance computing (HPC) systems. Nevertheless, leveraging these systems to execute complex big data processing workflows remains a significant challenge, especially for beginners. This work presents BiJuTy, a solution designed to bridge the accessibility gap for big data workflows on HPC systems within the Jupyter ecosystem. By providing an interactive and user-friendly interface, BiJuTy simplifies cluster lifecycle management and performance assessment, making it more accessible on HPC systems to beginners and experienced users alike. The solution is presented as an interactive interface that guides the user through the entire process, from setting up the cluster configuration to carrying out initial performance assessments. Additionally, the framework enables seamless management of multiple clusters directly within the Jupyter Notebook interface, eliminating the need to switch outside of working environment. The collection of performance metrics from various sources further simplifies the optimization workflow. Furthermore, an illustrative example is provided to demonstrate how BiJuTy can be deployed to optimize the performance of a big data processing application. This example showcases how the entire big data processing lifecycle can be iteratively executed and optimized in just a few clicks, helping to reach the goal of optimization easily and interactively. By facilitating such workflows, this work contributes in bringing the field of big data computing and high-performance computing one step closer to the goal of seamless interaction and usability.

</details>


### [65] [cuSBF: A Minimizer-Aware Bloom Filter for Genomic Sequence Data on Modern GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24417)
*Tim Dortmann,Markus Vieth,Bertil Schmidt*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 cuSBF，一个基于 CUDA 的 Super Bloom filter 库，通过扇区分片、协作共享内存平铺、warp 级分片共享和分段 warp 归约等架构感知设计，在 GPU 上实现了高效基因组 k-mer 索引。在 RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 上相比 cuCollections 插入和查询分别提升高达 9.1 倍和 7.7 倍，相比多线程 CPU SBF 加速 92-234 倍，并证明序列局部性而非原始带宽是 GPU 加速基因组索引的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Super Bloom filter (SBF) 在基因组 k-mer 索引中利用最小化子引导分片和 Findere 方案来利用重叠 k-mer 的冗余性，但这些特性导致高计算开销、严重寄存器压力和低效的 GPU 实现，亟需一种针对 GPU 架构优化的 SBF 实现。

Method: cuSBF 采用扇区分片、协作共享内存平铺、warp 级分片共享和分段 warp 归约等架构感知设计，将超 k-mer 局部性转化为可扩展的 GPU 并行性。

Result: 在 RTX PRO 6000 上，cuSBF 插入和查询分别比 cuCollections 快 9.1 倍和 7.7 倍，比多线程 CPU SBF 快 92-234 倍；比 GPU 动态 AMQ（Cuckoo、Two-Choice、Quotient filters）快 1.5-3400 倍；参数扫描确定 (s=28, m=16, H=4) 为 k=31 时的 Pareto 最优配置；即使在缓存外过滤器上也维持 85% 的流多处理器利用率。

Conclusion: cuSBF 证明了序列局部性而非原始带宽是 GPU 加速基因组索引的关键，其架构感知设计能够有效利用 GPU 资源实现高性能基因组 k-mer 索引。

Abstract: Efficient genomic k-mer indexing depends on approximate membership query (AMQ) structures that must deliver high throughput, low false-positive rates (FPR), and modest memory footprints. The Super Bloom filter (SBF) is attractive for this scenario because minimizer-guided sharding and the Findere scheme exploit the redundancy of overlapping k-mers. However, those same features cause high per-k-mer compute cost, severe register pressure, and irregular memory accesses, which hinder an effective GPU implementation. We present cuSBF, an open-source, header-only CUDA library that implements SBF for sequence-native workloads. cuSBF's design merges sectorized shards, cooperative shared-memory tiling, warp-level shard sharing, and segmented warp reductions, turning super-k-mer locality into scalable GPU parallelism. Across real genomic workloads on RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell and GH200 systems, cuSBF achieves the highest throughput among all evaluated sequence-capable baselines. On the RTX PRO 6000, it surpasses the cuCollections blocked Bloom filter baseline by up to 9.1x for insertion and 7.7x for query, while reaching up to 92x and 234x speedups over the multi-threaded CPU Super Bloom reference implementation. It also outperforms GPU-based dynamic AMQs (Cuckoo, Two-Choice, Quotient filters) by 1.5-3400x depending on workload characteristics. A parameter sweep identifies (s = 28, m = 16, H = 4) as Pareto-optimal for k = 31, yielding significantly lower FPR than cuCollections at matched memory budgets. Crucially, cuSBF's architecture-aware design sustains 85% streaming multiprocessor utilization even for out-of-cache filters - proving that sequence locality, not raw bandwidth, is the key to GPU-accelerated genomic indexing.

</details>


### [66] [CrossPool: Efficient Multi-LLM Serving for Cold MoE Models through KV-Cache and Weight Disaggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24506)
*Zhuoren Ye,Tianyu Wo,Dinghao Xue,Mingming Zhang,Yuchen Teng,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CrossPool是一种针对冷MoE模型的服务引擎，通过将FFN权重和KV-cache分离到两个GPU内存池中，利用共享KV-cache池按需分配活跃请求，结合KV-cache规划器、虚拟化器、层间流水线调度器和持久化内核等技术，显著提升了GPU内存利用率和对突发长上下文请求的支持，相比现有系统将P99 TBT降低了最多10.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务中大量稀疏MoE模型处于"冷"状态（请求稀疏），导致GPU内存利用率低：模型权重稳定且由模型决定，而KV-cache是瞬态的、由需求决定。为每个冷模型预留最坏情况下的KV容量会浪费内存。同时，单块GPU内存池中静态权重与动态KV-cache竞争资源，且冷模型低并发场景下KV-head有限的注意力只能利用复制的KV容量的一小部分，导致内存利用率低和长上下文支持弱。

Method: CrossPool将FFN权重和KV-cache分离到两个独立的GPU内存池：权重池整合多个冷模型的FFN权重，KV-cache池动态服务活跃请求并保持注意力局部化。核心组件包括：KV-cache规划器和虚拟化器、层间流水线调度器（隐藏隐藏状态传输）、持久化内核与控制降级技术（减少CPU-GPU控制开销）。

Result: CrossPool有效支持突发长上下文请求，相比基于kvcached的多LLM服务系统，P99 TBT（token间时延）降低最多10.4倍。

Conclusion: 通过分离权重和KV-cache为独立GPU内存池，结合高效的调度和内核优化，CrossPool解决了冷MoE模型服务中的内存利用率和长上下文支持问题，显著提升了系统性能。

Abstract: Emerging LLM services increasingly host many sparse MoE models, yet most models receive sparse requests and remain cold. This creates a GPU memory problem: model weights are stable and model-determined, while KV-cache is transient and demand-determined. Because cold models rarely reach peak KV-cache demand at the same time, reserving worst-case KV capacity per model wastes memory; a shared KV-cache pool can instead provision aggregate active demand. However, KV-cache sharing is not sufficient when weights and KV-cache remain in a monolithic GPU memory pool. Static weights compete with dynamic KV-cache, and KV-head-limited attention under cold, low-concurrency traffic exposes only a fraction of replicated KV capacity, leading to low GPU memory utilization and weak long-context support. We present CrossPool, a serving engine for cold MoE models that separates FFN weights and KV-cache into two GPU memory pools: a weights pool that consolidates FFN weights across cold models, and a KV-cache pool that dynamically serves active requests while keeping attention local to KV-cache. CrossPool combines a KV-cache planner and virtualizer, a layer-wise pipeline scheduler that hides hidden-state transfers, and persistent kernels with control lowering to reduce CPU-GPU control overhead. With efficient GPU memory pooling, CrossPool underpins bursty long-context requests and outperforms the state-of-the-art kvcached-based multi-LLM serving system, reducing P99 TBT by up to $10.4\times$.

</details>


### [67] [Unified Position-Invariant Random Access Through Two Compression Layers via Absolute-Offset Coordinates: A Bit-Perfect Device-Resident Proof](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24531)
*Yakiv Shavidze*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ACEAPEX 格式通过绝对偏移设计实现了在熵层和匹配层上的统一随机访问，支持使用单一坐标位完美解码任意数据块，并在 H100 上可展开至 25,344 个独立解析器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩数据的随机访问方案仅限于单层（熵层或匹配层），缺乏一种同时支持通过熵层和匹配层进行单一位置不变寻址的格式。

Method: ACEAPEX 采用绝对偏移设计：匹配层在编码时将每个反向引用解析为绝对位置，熵层按块独立应用；通过三阶段验证（关闭空缓冲区陷阱）确保位完美的端到端正确性。

Result: 单个 16KB 块通过 ANS 熵层和匹配层寻址耗时 0.334ms；在 H100 上可展开至 25,344 个独立解析器（远超顺序 LZ77 的并行能力）；在四种数据分布上验证了完整熵+匹配流水线。

Conclusion: ACEAPEX 的绝对偏移设计实现了熵层与匹配层的统一随机访问，具备位完美正确性和高并行度，但当前仅验证两层，三层推广尚为假设。

Abstract: Random access into compressed data is normally confined to a single layer. Entropy-layer methods (Recoil) seek within rANS by storing intermediate decoder states; dictionary/match-layer methods seek within LZ-style references. We are not aware of a format that supports a single position-invariant seek through both an entropy layer and a match layer addressed by one coordinate. We show that ACEAPEX's absolute-offset design provides exactly this: because the match layer resolves every back-reference to an absolute position at encode time, and the entropy layer is applied per block, an arbitrary block can be decoded through both layers using one coordinate, bit-perfect, in isolation. We prove this with a three-phase verification that closes the empty-buffer trap. The seek of one 16KB block through ANS-entropy and match completes in 0.334ms. We verify the full entropy+match pipeline end-to-end on four data profiles and characterize the hardware ceiling the format reaches: the absolute-offset structure unrolls to as many as 25,344 independent parsers on one H100, which sequential LZ77 cannot do. We state explicitly what is not claimed: this is a round-trip correctness proof, not a disk-archive format; throughput figures are match-phase; and the unified-seek result is demonstrated for two layers, with three-layer generalization left as a hypothesis. Code and the verification harness are in the project repository.

</details>


### [68] [Solvability of Approximate Agreement on Graphs and Simplicial Complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24666)
*Joel Rybicki,Yaroslav Verbitsky*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文给出了异步共享内存系统中图与单纯复形上近似一致问题的t-容错可解性的完整拓扑刻画，证明了团一致在图上t-容错可解当且仅当其团复形在同伦意义上是(t-1)-连通的，并回答了Ledent猜想等多个开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似一致问题是共识问题的离散松弛形式，尽管已有广泛研究，但异步系统中哪些图类可解仍不明确。本文旨在给出完整的拓扑刻画，解决多个相关开放问题。

Method: 采用拓扑学方法，通过分析图与单纯复形的连通性性质，建立t-容错可解性与团复形(t-1)-连通性之间的等价关系，并对不同变体（团一致、单声道一致、测地一致）进行比较分析。

Result: ①首次证明Ledent猜想（关于无等待可解性）；②团一致在图上t-容错可解当且仅当其团复形(t-1)-连通；③团一致与单声道一致可解图类相同，但单声道一致与测地一致之间存在可分离性；④在消息传递模型中得到新的异步近似一致弹性界和同步近似一致轮数下界。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了异步系统中图上近似一致问题的可解性边界，回答了多个开放问题，为分布式计算中图论与拓扑方法的交叉提供了新洞察。

Abstract: Approximate agreement tasks on graphs are discrete relaxations of consensus, where each process in a distributed system is given as input a vertex on a graph $G$, and processes have to output vertices that lie on a clique of $G$ contained in the convex hull of the input vertices. Although such tasks have been widely studied in a variety of models, graph classes and notions of convexity, it remains largely open for which classes of graphs these problems are solvable in asynchronous systems.
  In this work, we give a complete topological characterisation of the $t$-resilient solvability of approximate agreement on graphs and simplicial complexes in asynchronous shared-memory systems with read-write registers. As a result, we answer several open problems related to different variants of approximate agreement on graphs. For example, we give the first proof of Ledent's conjecture [PODC 2021] about the wait-free solvability of clique agreement.
  In fact, we show a more general result: clique agreement is $t$-resilient solvable on a graph $G$ if and only if its clique complex is $(t-1)$-connected in the homotopical sense. We also show that clique and monophonic agreement are solvable on the same class of graphs, but there exists a separation between monophonic and geodesic agreement, answering a question by Alistarh et al. [TCS 2023]. In the message-passing setting, our results imply new resilience bounds for asynchronous approximate agreement and round lower bounds for synchronous approximate agreement on graphs.

</details>


### [69] [A Natively Blocked, Device-Resident Algebraic Multigrid GPU Path in PETSc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24748)
*Mark F. Adams*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向弹性问题平滑聚合代数多重网格（AMG）的矩形块压缩格式与Kokkos后端实现，通过直接存储非零块、支持矩形分块（如粗网格块大小6 vs 细网格块大小3），在GPU上避免了标量展开，显著提升了内存效率和计算性能。在A100 GPU上，块格式在128^3网格（8 GPU）避免了cuSPARSE的显存溢出，并在27 GPU上实现V-cycle加速1.24倍、SpMV加速1.42倍、粗算子重算加速1.80倍，64 GPU时后者达2.27倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU上的AMG实现使用标量稀疏矩阵格式，未利用弹性问题中自然的块结构（非零元以稠密块形式存在），导致索引数据冗余、算术强度低；同时现有阻塞内核（cuSPARSE、Kokkos Kernels）要求行列块大小相同，而弹性AMG的粗网格（刚体模态，块大小6）与细网格（块大小3）块大小不同，无法直接应用。因此需要一种支持矩形块的高效GPU实现。

Method: 在PETSc中通过Kokkos后端实现了可移植的矩形块压缩矩阵类型，包含：（1）矩形块内核（支持不等行列块大小）；（2）平滑聚合设置全过程直接操作块格式，无需转换为标量形式；（3）层次复用中的Galerkin粗算子重算和V-cycle均保持块格式在设备端驻留；（4）实现原生块格式的离处理器延拓算子收集（PetscSF）和块COO组装路径。

Result: 在A100 GPU上，128^3网格（630万未知量，8 GPU）时，cuSPARSE Galerkin乘积因显存不足失败，而块格式可正常运行；与Kokkos Kernels标量路径相比，块格式在27 GPU时V-cycle快1.24倍、SpMV快1.42倍、粗算子重算快1.80倍，64 GPU时粗算子重算加速比达2.27倍。

Conclusion: 所提出的矩形块格式与Kokkos后端实现有效解决了弹性问题AMG的GPU内存瓶颈和性能问题，在小规模GPU上性能持平，在大规模GPU上性能显著提升，尤其粗算子重算环节加速效果最明显。

Abstract: Smoothed-aggregation algebraic multigrid (AMG) is widely used for the linear systems arising from finite-element discretizations of vector PDEs such as elasticity, but its GPU implementations have used scalar sparse matrix formats. These problems carry a natural block structure: matrix nonzeros occur in dense bs x bs blocks sharing one column index, so storing the blocks directly removes most of the index data and raises the arithmetic intensity of the bandwidth-bound kernels that dominate AMG on the GPU. Existing blocked GPU kernels (cuSPARSE, Kokkos Kernels) require equal row and column block sizes, but AMG for elasticity is rectangular-blocked: the near-null space of rigid-body modes makes the coarse block size (6 in 3D) differ from the fine (3), so the prolongator and the Galerkin triple product mix block sizes. We add a portable, Kokkos-backed blocked matrix type to PETSc with rectangular-block kernels, and make every step of the smoothed-aggregation setup operate on the block format directly, with no expansion to scalar form on the coarsening path. The two phases that recur when the hierarchy is reused across solves -- the Galerkin coarse-operator recompute (A_c = P^T A P) and the V-cycle -- are kept resident on the device in blocks, via a native blocked off-process prolongator gather over a PetscSF and a new blocked COO assembly path for dense rectangular blocks. On A100 GPUs for 3D elasticity, the cuSPARSE Galerkin product runs out of GPU memory on a 128^3 grid (6.3M unknowns) packed onto 8 GPUs, where the blocked format fits; the native Kokkos Kernels scalar path also fits, but with a much heavier Galerkin product. Where the formats run, the blocked format is at parity on one GPU and faster at scale: at 27 GPUs it is 1.24x faster on the V-cycle, 1.42x on SpMV, and 1.80x on the coarse-operator recompute, reaching 2.27x on the latter at 64 GPUs.

</details>

### [70] [RIFT-Bench: Dynamic Red-teaming For Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23927)
*Yarin Yerushalmi Levi,Roy Betser,Amit Giloni,Lidor Erez,Itay Gershon,Oren Rachmil,Sindhu Padakandla,Roman Vainshtein*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RIFT-Bench，一种基于图表示驱动的动态红队方法论，用于对异质智能体AI系统进行统一安全评估。该方法包含自动化的发现（提取系统结构）和扫描（部署自适应对抗攻击）两个阶段，在45个智能体系统上验证了其泛化性和有效性，并支持对防御策略的直接评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的智能体AI系统正演变为自主决策系统，暴露了超越传统LLM漏洞的攻击向量。现有安全评估通常局限于特定实现或领域，缺乏跨异质系统的统一比较框架。

Method: 提出RIFT-Bench，采用图表示驱动方法。包含两个自动化阶段：（1）Discovery阶段，提取系统结构；（2）Scanning阶段，部署自适应对抗攻击并生成综合评估报告。利用分层表示和动态可调的对抗探测，覆盖多样攻击向量和目标。

Result: 在45个涵盖广泛实现类型的智能体系统上进行验证，证明该方法能有效泛化到异质智能体架构，并支持对防御策略的直接评估。

Conclusion: RIFT-Bench提供了一种可扩展的安全评估基础框架，能够统一评估不同架构的智能体AI系统，并支持防御策略评估。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems powered by large language models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving into autonomous decision-making systems, exposing attack vectors beyond those of traditional LLM vulnerabilities. Existing security evaluations are often tied to specific implementations or domains, limiting unified comparison across heterogeneous systems. To address this gap, we introduce RIFT-Bench, a graph representation-driven methodology for dynamic red-teaming that enables unified evaluations across diverse agentic architectures. Building on a novel hierarchical representation, RIFT-Bench operates in two automated phases: Discovery, which extracts system structure, and Scanning, which deploys adaptive adversarial attacks and produces a comprehensive evaluation report.
